Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on nonlinear structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 113. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 770 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the nonlinear decomposition. By minimality, the element a^113 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 770-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 217.3 (Optimality Condition)
